one-shot observation layout, so a one-shot checkpoint is the starting point and
step 1 is still a plain one-shot policy. Training backpropagates the goal
distance through a K-step rollout in the frozen dynamics ensemble (final step
weight 1, earlier steps `intermediate_weight`).

On task v2 the first step of a K=2 fine-tune is the best pure policy so far
(docs/experiments/2026-10-17-task-v2.md); evaluation re-renders after every step.

    .venv/bin/python -m rl.closed_loop --init out/rl_mb_v2/r2_seed0/checkpoint.pt \\
        --data out/rl_mb_v2/data_r0b.npz,out/rl_mb_v2/data_r1.npz,out/rl_mb_v2/data_r2.npz \\
        --out out/rl_mb_v2/cl2_r2_seed0 --task-version v2
"""
import argparse
import collections
import json
import os

import numpy as np
import torch

import goals
from rl import model_based as mb
from rl.baselines import apply_controllable

N = mb.N_CLASSES


def remaining_goal(goal, delta, weights=None):
    """Batched: what is left of `goal` (B x 4n) after the cumulative change `delta`
    (B x STATE_SIZE) from the start. Mentioned targets shrink by the progress;
    an unmentioned class that drifted past KEEP_TOLERANCE gets an undo target;
    classes with weight 0 (task v2 occluders) stay free."""
    tol = goals.KEEP_TOLERANCE
    c, b = delta[:, :N], delta[:, N + 1:2 * N + 1]
    d, m, e, mb_ = goal[:, :N], goal[:, N:2 * N], goal[:, 2 * N:3 * N], goal[:, 3 * N:]
    drifted = (m == 0) & (c.abs() > tol)
    new_d = torch.where(m > 0, d - c, torch.where(drifted, -c, torch.zeros_like(d)))
    if weights is not None:
        new_d = torch.where(weights[:, :N] == 0, torch.zeros_like(d), new_d)
    new_m = torch.where(drifted, torch.ones_like(m), m)
    new_e = torch.where(mb_ > 0, e - b, torch.zeros_like(e))
    return torch.cat([new_d, new_m, new_e, mb_], 1)


def remaining_goal_for(goal, start_agg, current_agg, weights=None) -> np.ndarray:
    """`remaining_goal` for one real episode."""
    delta = torch.as_tensor(mb.state_vector(current_agg) - mb.state_vector(start_agg))[None]
    w = None
    if weights:
        w = torch.tensor([[weights.get(c, 1.0) for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES]], dtype=torch.float32)
    return remaining_goal(torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(goal), dtype=torch.float32)[None], delta, w)[0].numpy()


def finetune(policy, ensemble, data, train_starts, steps: int = 20_000, horizon: int = 2, seed: int = 0,
             batch: int = 256, learning_rate: float = 1e-4, intermediate_weight: float = 0.2,
             balance_kinds: bool = False, log=print):
    """Train `policy` in place through `horizon`-step rollouts in the frozen `ensemble`.
    `balance_kinds` draws every instruction kind equally often instead of in the
    grammar's mix (absolute and brightness are 10 % each)."""
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    T = mb._tensor
    goal_all, weights_all = T(data["goal"]), T(data["weights"])
    hist, solo, ctrl0, s0 = (T(data[k]) for k in ("hist", "solo", "ctrl", "s0"))
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(policy.net.parameters(), lr=learning_rate)
    policy.net.train()
    per_start = data["goal"].shape[1]
    pairs = np.array([(s, i) for s in train_starts for i in range(per_start)])
    p = None
    if balance_kinds:
        kinds = data["kind"][pairs[:, 0], pairs[:, 1]]
        names, inverse, counts = np.unique(kinds, return_inverse=True, return_counts=True)
        p = 1.0 / counts[inverse]
        p /= p.sum()
    model_attainment = None
    for step in range(steps):
        chosen = pairs[rng.choice(len(pairs), batch, p=p)]
        starts, pick = torch.as_tensor(chosen[:, 0]), torch.as_tensor(chosen[:, 1])
        goal, weights = goal_all[starts, pick], weights_all[starts, pick]
        start_distance = mb.goal_distance(goal, weights, torch.zeros(batch, mb.STATE_SIZE))
        keep = start_distance > 1e-6
        ctrl, state = ctrl0[starts], s0[starts]
        loss = penalty = 0.0
        for t in range(horizon):
            obs = mb.policy_observation(remaining_goal(goal, state - s0[starts], weights),
                                        hist[starts], state, solo[starts], ctrl)
            action = (ctrl + policy.action_tensor(obs)).clamp(-1, 1) - ctrl
            pred = ensemble(torch.cat([hist[starts], solo[starts], ctrl, state], 1), action)
            mean = pred.mean(0)
            state, ctrl = state + mean[:, :mb.STATE_SIZE], ctrl + action
            normalised = mb.goal_distance(goal, weights, state - s0[starts]) / start_distance.clamp_min(1e-6)
            loss = loss + (1.0 if t == horizon - 1 else intermediate_weight) * normalised.clamp(max=3.0)
            penalty = penalty + pred[:, :, :mb.STATE_SIZE].std(0).mean(1) + torch.sigmoid(mean[:, mb.STATE_SIZE])
        total = (loss + penalty)[keep].mean()
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        total.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        if step % 1000 == 999:
            model_attainment = float((1 - normalised[keep]).median().detach())
            log(f"[closed_loop] step {step + 1}: model-predicted final attainment {model_attainment:+.3f}")
    policy.net.eval()
    return policy, {"model_predicted_median": model_attainment}


def rollout(policy, env, horizon: int = 2) -> list:
    """Run `policy` for `horizon` steps from the episode `env` (a `OneShotEnv`
    after `reset`, or frozen into an episode) is in, re-rendering and
    re-observing the remaining goal after each step. Returns the attainment
    after every step, measured from the episode start; the first is the
    one-shot score. Leaves `env._params` at the final transfer function."""
    from rl.oneshot_env import build_observation
    if env.action_mode != "residual":
        raise ValueError("closed-loop rollout needs a residual-action policy")
    model, instruction, start_agg, layers = env._model, env._instruction, env._start_agg, env._active_layers
    solo = env._solo_max_log(env._volume, model)
    params, agg, scores = np.asarray(env._start_params, dtype=np.float64).copy(), start_agg, []
    for _ in range(horizon):
        ctrl = np.asarray(env._controllable_values(params))
        goal = remaining_goal_for(instruction["goal"], start_agg, agg, instruction.get("weights"))
        action, _ = policy.predict(build_observation(goal, model.histogram, agg, solo, ctrl))
        params = apply_controllable(params, np.clip(ctrl + action, -1, 1))
        agg = goals.aggregate(env._features(params), layers)
        scores.append(env._attainment(agg))
    env._params = params
    return scores


def evaluate(policy, horizon: int = 2, episodes: int = 200, seed_base: int = mb.VALIDATION_SEED_BASE,
             split: str = "val", task_version: str = "v1", volume_ids=None, model_for_volume=None) -> dict:
    """The policy alone on real renders (`rollout`), per kind and per step
    ("t1" is the one-shot score)."""
    import datasets
    from rl.oneshot_env import OneShotEnv
    ids = volume_ids if volume_ids is not None else datasets.volumes_for_split(split)
    kwargs = {} if model_for_volume is None else {"model_for_volume": model_for_volume}
    env = OneShotEnv(ids, **kwargs, **mb.ENV_KWARGS, task_version=task_version)
    by_kind, rows = collections.defaultdict(lambda: collections.defaultdict(list)), []
    for seed in range(seed_base, seed_base + episodes):
        env.reset(seed=seed)
        row = {"seed": seed, "kind": env._instruction["kind"], "solvable": env._solvable}
        for t, score in enumerate(rollout(policy, env, horizon), start=1):
            row[f"t{t}"] = score
            by_kind[row["kind"]][f"t{t}"].append(score)
            by_kind["ALL"][f"t{t}"].append(score)
        rows.append(row)
    medians = {k: {t: float(np.median(v)) for t, v in steps.items()} for k, steps in sorted(by_kind.items())}
    return {"medians": medians, "rows": rows}


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--init", required=True, help="model-based checkpoint.pt (policy and dynamics)")
    parser.add_argument("--data", required=True, help="comma-separated .npz files from `rl.model_based collect`")
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--horizon", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=20_000)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=1e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--intermediate-weight", type=float, default=0.2)
    parser.add_argument("--balance-kinds", action="store_true",
                        help="draw every instruction kind equally often")
    parser.add_argument("--eval-episodes", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--task-version", choices=goals.TASK_VERSIONS, default="v1")
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    torch.set_num_threads(args.threads)
    data = mb.load_data(args.data.split(","))
    train_starts, _ = mb.split_starts(len(data["s0"]))
    ensemble = mb.load_dynamics(args.init)
    for parameter in ensemble.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad_(False)
    policy = mb.ModelBasedPolicy.load(args.init)
    policy, report = finetune(policy, ensemble, data, train_starts, steps=args.steps, horizon=args.horizon,
                              seed=args.seed, learning_rate=args.learning_rate,
                              intermediate_weight=args.intermediate_weight,
                              balance_kinds=args.balance_kinds)
    policy.metadata = {"init": args.init, "data": args.data.split(","), "closed_loop_horizon": args.horizon,
                       "train_steps": args.steps, "seed": args.seed, "learning_rate": args.learning_rate,
                       "intermediate_weight": args.intermediate_weight,
                       "balance_kinds": args.balance_kinds, "task_version": args.task_version}
    os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)
    if args.eval_episodes:
        result = evaluate(policy, horizon=args.horizon, episodes=args.eval_episodes,
                          task_version=args.task_version)
        report["val"] = result["medians"]
        for kind, steps in result["medians"].items():
            print(f"[closed_loop] {kind:10s} " + "  ".join(f"{t} {v:+.3f}" for t, v in steps.items()))
        with open(os.path.join(args.out, "eval_closed_loop.json"), "w") as stream:
            json.dump(result, stream, indent=1)
    print(f"[closed_loop] wrote {policy.save(args.out, ensemble, report)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### rl/keep_repair.py
```
"""Inference-time keep repair: undo the policy's side effects on classes the
instruction never mentioned, where undoing them costs (almost) nothing.

`goals.distance` lets every unmentioned class drift up to KEEP_TOLERANCE for
free, measured from *that command's* start. One command at a time that is
harmless; chained in the viewer, each command starting from the previous
answer, the free drift adds up (2026-10-22: after 7 commands the heart's
brightness had fallen 0.36 and rendered black, vessels +0.46 and the
unlabelled shell +0.37 in log visibility, none of them ever named).

Peak <-> class: one peak per canonical goal class, at index
`transfer.ANATOMICAL_PEAK_INDEX[c]`, i.e. peak i belongs to
`anatomy.CANONICAL_PEAK_ORDER[i]` (lungs, soft, liver, kidneys, spleen, heart,
vessels, skeleton). Centres are fixed (neither policy nor search moves them),
so `commands._find_or_create_peak`'s nearest-centre lookup lands on the same
index for every anatomical class. In label-scoped rendering a voxel labelled c
is shaded by peak c alone; unlabelled voxels by the skeleton, lungs and soft
peaks (`transfer.UNLABELLED_PEAKS`). Those three are still just their class's
peak here: reverting an unmentioned one also moves the unlabelled shell back
toward the start, which is the point, and when the shell matters to the goal
(an occluder, or "other" is charged) the attainment check below keeps it.
"""
import numpy as np

import goals
import transfer

PEAK_CLASS = {index: goal_class for goal_class, index in transfer.ANATOMICAL_PEAK_INDEX.items()}
P = transfer.PARAMS_PER_PEAK
DEFAULT_EPS = 0.01


def mentioned_classes(goal) -> set:
    """Goal classes the goal vector mentions, for visibility (m) or
    brightness (n). Task v2/v3's occluders are marked mentioned with weight 0
    (`goals.occlusion_aware`), so they count as mentioned here too."""
    goal = np.asarray(goal, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(goals.GOAL_CLASSES)
    return {c for i, c in enumerate(goals.GOAL_CLASSES) if goal[n + i] or goal[3 * n + i]}


def occluder_classes(goal, weights) -> set:
    """Mentioned classes the instruction weighs 0: task v2/v3's occluders of a
    target (`goals.occlusion_aware`). No `weights`, no occluders. "other" is
    not a peak and never in the set."""
    if not weights:
        return set()
    return {c for c in mentioned_classes(goal) if weights.get(c, 1.0) == 0.0}


def keep_repair(model, goal, start_params, params, layers=None, weights=None, eps=DEFAULT_EPS,
                start_agg=None, include_occluders=False):
    """Revert unmentioned peaks of `params` to `start_params` where that
    costs at most `eps` attainment. Returns (params, n_evals).

    Every peak whose class the goal does not mention (`mentioned_classes`)
    and whose 6 values differ from the start is a candidate, tried once, the
    largest change (L2 over its 6 values) first. A revert stays if the
    attainment -- `goals.attainment(goal, start_agg, ., weights)`, the same
    score the policy and search are measured with -- is at least the
    proposal's minus `eps`. The bar is the proposal's, not the previous
    revert's, so the total loss is bounded by `eps`, not `eps` per revert.

    `start_agg` is the aggregate at `start_params`; given, it is reused,
    otherwise measured. `n_evals` counts the visibility evaluations this call
    made: the start (unless given), the proposal, one per candidate. A goal
    already met at the start (zero start distance, attainment undefined)
    returns `params` unchanged.

    `include_occluders=True` then tries the peaks of the instruction's
    occluders (`occluder_classes`: mentioned, weight 0) in a second pass on
    that result, same order, against the better of the proposal's and the
    first pass's score minus `eps`. The first pass is exactly what the call
    returns without the flag, so the answer stays within `eps` of both the
    proposal and the plain keep repair (tried first, an occluder revert would
    change what the later reverts gain: up to -0.17 against keep on v3c val).
    Occluders are mentioned only so the keep term does not charge fading
    them, so `distance` sees them solely through what they hide: when the
    fade is what reveals the target, reverting it costs the target's term and
    fails the bar; when it bought nothing, the revert is free and stays.
    Classes with a non-zero weight are never touched."""
    start_params = np.asarray(start_params, dtype=np.float64)
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    kwargs = {} if weights is None else {"weights": weights}
    n_evals = 0

    def measure(p):
        nonlocal n_evals
        n_evals += 1
        return goals.aggregate(goals.features(model, p, layers), layers)

    if start_agg is None:
        start_agg = measure(start_params)
    if goals.distance(goal, start_agg, start_agg, **kwargs) <= 0.0:
        return params, n_evals

    def score(p):
        return goals.attainment(goal, start_agg, measure(p), **kwargs)

    def candidates(classes):
        changes = []
        for index, goal_class in PEAK_CLASS.items():
            if goal_class not in classes:
                continue
            span = slice(index * P, (index + 1) * P)
            size = float(np.linalg.norm(params[span] - start_params[span]))
            if size > 0.0:
                changes.append((size, index, span))
        return [span for _size, _index, span in sorted(changes, key=lambda item: (-item[0], item[1]))]

    def revert(spans, bar):
        """Each span reverted in turn where the score stays >= bar; returns
        the score of the last accepted state (None if none was accepted)."""
        nonlocal params
        last = None
        for span in spans:
            candidate = params.copy()
            candidate[span] = start_params[span]
            value = score(candidate)
            if value >= bar:
                params, last = candidate, value
        return last

    unmentioned = set(PEAK_CLASS.values()) - mentioned_classes(goal)
    proposal_score = score(params)
    bar = proposal_score - eps
    kept_score = revert(candidates(unmentioned), bar)
    if include_occluders:
        reached = proposal_score if kept_score is None else max(proposal_score, kept_score)
        revert(candidates(occluder_classes(goal, weights)), reached - eps)
    return params, n_evals
```

### rl/model_based.py
```
"""Model-based RL for the one-shot task: learn what a transfer-function change
does to the measured visibility, then train the policy through that model with
the exact goal metric.

Why not SAC: model-free SAC on `OneShotEnv` (E3-E9, docs/experiments/) stays at
a median attainment of about 0. Its critic has to learn instruction x physics
jointly from a scalar reward. Here the two are split:

1. **Dynamics.** An ensemble of MLPs maps (volume histogram, solo_max ceilings,
   start controllable values, start state, residual action) to the change in
   `state_vector` -- per-class log10 visibility (plus "other"), brightness and
   coverage -- and a "useless" logit. The renderer's physics does not depend on
   the instruction, so random TF changes on training-split starts teach it
   (`collect`).
2. **Policy.** An MLP with the one-shot observation layout
   (`rl.oneshot_env.build_observation`) and residual actions, trained by
   backpropagating `goal_distance` -- a torch twin of `goals.distance` -- through
   the frozen ensemble (SVG(0)/Dreamer-style actor training with a known
   reward). An ensemble-disagreement penalty keeps it where the model is sure.
3. **Dyna.** Rendering the policy's own proposals and refitting the model
   closes most of the model-exploitation gap (`collect --policy`).

`ModelBasedPolicy` has SB3's `predict` and space attributes, so `policy.py`
(the viewer) and `rl.vis_eval` run it like a SAC checkpoint.

    .venv/bin/python -m rl.model_based collect --out out/rl_mb/data_r0.npz --starts 3000
    .venv/bin/python -m rl.model_based train --data out/rl_mb/data_r0.npz --out out/rl_mb/r0_seed0
    .venv/bin/python -m rl.model_based collect --out out/rl_mb/data_r1.npz --starts 1500 \\
        --seed-base 2000000 --policy out/rl_mb/r0_seed0/checkpoint.pt
    .venv/bin/python -m rl.model_based train --data out/rl_mb/data_r0.npz,out/rl_mb/data_r1.npz \\
        --out out/rl_mb/r1_seed0
"""
import argparse
import collections
import json
import math
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
import time

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

import goals
from rl.baselines import CONTROLLABLE, apply_controllable
from rl.oneshot_env import (ACTION_SIZE, OBSERVATION_BOUND, OBSERVATION_SIZE, V9_POLICY_VERSION,
                            observation_metadata)

N_CLASSES = len(goals.GOAL_CLASSES)
HISTOGRAM_SIZE = 16
# log10 visibility per goal class + "other", brightness per goal class, coverage
STATE_SIZE = (N_CLASSES + 1) + N_CLASSES + 1
CONTEXT_SIZE = HISTOGRAM_SIZE + N_CLASSES + ACTION_SIZE + STATE_SIZE
CHECKPOINT_NAME = "checkpoint.pt"
METADATA_NAME = "metadata.json"
ENV_KWARGS = {"policy_version": V9_POLICY_VERSION, "action_mode": "residual", "reward_mode": "target"}

# Random actions for the dynamics data: dense small moves, sparse large moves of
# 1-4 groups (one organ's peak, say), and no-ops -- the policy's proposals fall
# in all three regimes.
DENSE_SHARE, SPARSE_SHARE = 0.4, 0.5
ACTIONS_PER_START = 20
INSTRUCTIONS_PER_START = 8
POLICY_NOISE_MAX = 0.3
VALIDATION_SEED_BASE = 50_000


def state_vector(agg: dict) -> np.ndarray:
    """An aggregated feature dict (`goals.aggregate`) as `STATE_SIZE` floats,
    visibility in the log10 units `goals.progress` measures change in."""
    log_vis = [math.log10(agg["vis"].get(c, 0.0) + goals.EPSILON)
               for c in list(goals.GOAL_CLASSES) + ["other"]]
    bright = [agg["bright"][c] for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES]
    return np.array(log_vis + bright + [agg["coverage"]], dtype=np.float32)


def mlp(inputs: int, outputs: int, hidden: int = 256, depth: int = 3) -> nn.Sequential:
    layers, width = [], inputs
    for _ in range(depth):
        layers += [nn.Linear(width, hidden), nn.SiLU()]
        width = hidden
    return nn.Sequential(*layers, nn.Linear(width, outputs))


class DynamicsEnsemble(nn.Module):
    """K MLPs: (context, residual action) -> (delta state, useless logit), K x B x (STATE_SIZE + 1)."""

    def __init__(self, members: int = 5):
        super().__init__()
        self.nets = nn.ModuleList(mlp(CONTEXT_SIZE + ACTION_SIZE, STATE_SIZE + 1) for _ in range(members))
        self.register_buffer("mean", torch.zeros(CONTEXT_SIZE + ACTION_SIZE))
        self.register_buffer("scale", torch.ones(CONTEXT_SIZE + ACTION_SIZE))

    def forward(self, context, action):
        x = (torch.cat([context, action], -1) - self.mean) / self.scale
        return torch.stack([net(x) for net in self.nets])


def goal_distance(goal, weights, delta):
    """`goals.distance` in torch, batched: `goal` B x 4n, `weights` B x n or
    B x (n + 1) (1.0 where the instruction has none; the optional last column is
    weights["other"], task v2's occlusion exemption), `delta` B x STATE_SIZE (the
    change from the start state, so `delta = 0` is the start's own distance)."""
    n = N_CLASSES
    other_weight = weights[:, n] if weights.shape[1] > n else 1.0
    weights = weights[:, :n]
    c, c_other, b = delta[:, :n], delta[:, n], delta[:, n + 1:2 * n + 1]
    d, m, e, mb = goal[:, :n], goal[:, n:2 * n], goal[:, 2 * n:3 * n], goal[:, 3 * n:4 * n]
    total = (m * weights * (c - d).abs()).sum(1) + goals.KAPPA * (mb * weights * (b - e).abs()).sum(1)
    total = total + goals.LAMBDA_KEEP * ((1 - m) * torch.relu(c.abs() - goals.KEEP_TOLERANCE)).sum(1)
    total = total + goals.LAMBDA_KEEP * goals.KAPPA * (
        (1 - mb) * torch.relu(b.abs() - goals.KEEP_TOLERANCE)).sum(1)
    return total + goals.LAMBDA_KEEP * other_weight * torch.relu(c_other.abs() - goals.KEEP_TOLERANCE)


def policy_observation(goal, histogram, start_state, solo_max_log, controllable):
    """`rl.oneshot_env.build_observation`'s layout from batched tensors."""
    n = N_CLASSES
    obs = torch.cat([goal, histogram, start_state[:, :n], start_state[:, n + 1:2 * n + 1],
                     solo_max_log, controllable, start_state[:, -1:]], -1)
    return obs.clamp(-OBSERVATION_BOUND, OBSERVATION_BOUND)


class _Space:
    def __init__(self, shape):
        self.shape = shape


class ModelBasedPolicy:
    """The trained actor behind SB3's interface (`predict`, `observation_space`,
    `action_space`): observation -> residual action in [-1, 1]."""

    def __init__(self, net: nn.Module, metadata: dict = None):
        self.net = net.eval()
        self.metadata = dict(metadata or {})
        self.observation_space = _Space((OBSERVATION_SIZE,))
        self.action_space = _Space((ACTION_SIZE,))

    @staticmethod
    def new_net(hidden: int = 256, depth: int = 2) -> nn.Module:
        return mlp(OBSERVATION_SIZE, ACTION_SIZE, hidden=hidden, depth=depth)

    def action_tensor(self, observation):
        return torch.tanh(self.net(observation.clamp(-OBSERVATION_BOUND, OBSERVATION_BOUND)))

    def predict(self, observation, deterministic: bool = True):
        obs = torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(observation, dtype=np.float32))
        with torch.no_grad():
            action = self.action_tensor(obs.reshape(-1, OBSERVATION_SIZE)).numpy()
        return (action[0] if np.ndim(observation) == 1 else action), None

    def save(self, out_dir: str, dynamics: DynamicsEnsemble = None, report: dict = None) -> str:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
        path = os.path.join(out_dir, CHECKPOINT_NAME)
        torch.save({"policy": self.net.state_dict(),
                    "arch": {"hidden": self.net[0].out_features, "depth": (len(self.net) - 1) // 2},
                    "dynamics": None if dynamics is None else dynamics.state_dict(),
                    "metadata": self.metadata}, path)
        # rl.oneshot_env.load_policy_metadata reads this, as for a SAC run.
        with open(os.path.join(out_dir, METADATA_NAME), "w") as stream:
            task_version = self.metadata.get("task_version", "v1")
            json.dump({**observation_metadata(**ENV_KWARGS, task_version=task_version), "algorithm": "model_based",
                       **self.metadata, **({"report": report} if report else {})}, stream, indent=2)
        return path

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: str) -> "ModelBasedPolicy":
        payload = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
        net = cls.new_net(**payload.get("arch", {}))
        net.load_state_dict(payload["policy"])
        policy = cls(net, payload.get("metadata"))
        if payload.get("dynamics") is not None:
            # Kept with the policy so a caller can plan through it (`plan`).
            policy.dynamics = DynamicsEnsemble()
            policy.dynamics.load_state_dict(payload["dynamics"])
            policy.dynamics.eval().requires_grad_(False)
        return policy


def load_dynamics(path: str) -> DynamicsEnsemble:
    payload = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    ensemble = DynamicsEnsemble()
    ensemble.load_state_dict(payload["dynamics"])
    return ensemble.eval()


# --- planning ---------------------------------------------------------------

# Test-time planning: the policy's residual action refined by gradient descent
# on the training loss (`train_policy`'s normalised goal distance + beta x
# ensemble disagreement + useless-render probability) for this one
# instruction, through the learned model -- no renders. Restarts are the
# policy action plus noise of 0-0.3. Task v3, 300 val episodes, v3 seed 2
# (median attainment): policy 0.51, planned 0.66; 300 steps 0.61 and 50 steps
# 0.61 (more steps exploit model error), beta 0.3 0.59 / 3 0.64, a no-op
# start 0.60 (docs/experiments/2026-10-19-task-v3.md).
PLAN_STEPS, PLAN_RESTARTS, PLAN_BETA, PLAN_LR, PLAN_NOISE = 100, 16, 1.0, 0.05, 0.3


def weight_row(weights) -> list:
    """An instruction's `weights` dict as the n + 1 floats `goal_distance` takes."""
    weights = weights or {}
    return [weights.get(c, 1.0) for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES] + [weights.get("other", 1.0)]


def plan_loss(ensemble, context, goal, weights, residual, start, beta: float = PLAN_BETA):
    """`plan`'s per-row objective (`train_policy`'s loss): model-predicted goal
    distance over the start's, + `beta` x disagreement + P(useless render)."""
    pred = ensemble(context, residual)
    mean = pred.mean(0)
    return (goal_distance(goal, weights, mean[:, :STATE_SIZE]) / start
            + beta * pred[:, :, :STATE_SIZE].std(0).mean(1) + torch.sigmoid(mean[:, STATE_SIZE]))


def plan(ensemble, goal, weights, histogram, solo_max_log, controllable, start_agg, action,
         steps: int = PLAN_STEPS, restarts: int = PLAN_RESTARTS, beta: float = PLAN_BETA,
         lr: float = PLAN_LR, seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:
    """The residual `action` (as the policy proposes it, relative to
    `controllable`) refined through `ensemble` for one instruction (`goal`,
    `weights` dict or None) from `start_agg`. Returns the residual actually
    applied, `clip(controllable + a) - controllable`, of the restart with the
    lowest model loss. An instruction already at its goal returns `action`."""
    ctrl = _tensor(controllable)
    goal_row = _tensor(goal)[None]
    weight = _tensor(weight_row(weights))[None]
    context = _tensor(np.concatenate([np.asarray(histogram, np.float32), np.asarray(solo_max_log, np.float32),
                                      np.asarray(controllable, np.float32), state_vector(start_agg)]))[None]
    applied = (ctrl + _tensor(np.clip(action, -1, 1))).clamp(-1, 1) - ctrl
    start = goal_distance(goal_row, weight, torch.zeros(1, STATE_SIZE))[0]
    if float(start) <= 1e-6:
        return applied.numpy()
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    noise = torch.randn(restarts, ACTION_SIZE, generator=generator) * torch.linspace(0, PLAN_NOISE, restarts)[:, None]
    candidate = (applied[None] + noise).requires_grad_(True)
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam([candidate], lr=lr)
    goal_row, weight, context = (x.expand(restarts, -1) for x in (goal_row, weight, context))

    def loss_of(residual):
        residual = (ctrl + residual).clamp(-1, 1) - ctrl
        return plan_loss(ensemble, context, goal_row, weight, residual, start, beta), residual

    with torch.enable_grad():
        for _ in range(steps):
            loss, _ = loss_of(candidate)
            # Only the candidate's gradient: the ensemble's weights stay untouched.
            candidate.grad, = torch.autograd.grad(loss.sum(), [candidate])
            optimizer.step()
    with torch.no_grad():
        loss, residual = loss_of(candidate)
    return residual[int(loss.argmin())].numpy()


# --- data -------------------------------------------------------------------

def _collect_chunk(args):
    seeds, actions_per_start, policy_path, volume_ids, start_variety, task_version = args
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    from rl.oneshot_train import build_env
    env = build_env(volume_ids=volume_ids, **ENV_KWARGS, balance_classes=True, start_variety=start_variety,
                    task_version=task_version)
    policy = ModelBasedPolicy.load(policy_path) if policy_path else None
    return collect_starts(env, seeds, actions_per_start, policy)


def collect_starts(env, seeds, actions_per_start: int = ACTIONS_PER_START, policy=None) -> dict:
    """For each seeded start of `env` (a `OneShotEnv`): render `actions_per_start`
    TF changes and record the resulting `state_vector`, and sample
    `INSTRUCTIONS_PER_START` real instructions for policy training. With
    `policy`, the first half of the actions are its proposals plus noise
    (Dyna: data where the policy actually acts)."""
    out = {k: [] for k in ("hist", "solo", "ctrl", "s0", "act", "s1", "useless", "goal", "weights", "kind",
                           "volume")}
    for seed in seeds:
        obs, _ = env.reset(seed=int(seed))
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        ctrl = np.asarray(env._controllable_values(env._start_params))
        actions, states, useless = [], [], []
        for j in range(actions_per_start):
            draw = rng.random()
            if policy is not None and j < actions_per_start // 2:
                action, _ = policy.predict(obs)
                action = action + rng.normal(0.0, rng.uniform(0.0, POLICY_NOISE_MAX), ACTION_SIZE)
            elif draw < DENSE_SHARE:
                action = rng.uniform(-1, 1, ACTION_SIZE) * rng.uniform(0.05, 0.8)
            elif draw < DENSE_SHARE + SPARSE_SHARE:
                action = np.zeros(ACTION_SIZE)
                picked = rng.choice(ACTION_SIZE, rng.integers(1, 5), replace=False)
                action[picked] = rng.uniform(-1, 1, len(picked))
            else:
                action = np.zeros(ACTION_SIZE)
            action = np.clip(ctrl + np.clip(action, -1, 1), -1, 1) - ctrl   # the residual actually applied
            raw = goals.features(env._model, apply_controllable(env._start_params, ctrl + action))
            actions.append(action)
            states.append(state_vector(goals.aggregate(raw)))
            useless.append(goals.is_useless(raw))
        goal_rows, weight_rows, kinds = [], [], []
        for _ in range(INSTRUCTIONS_PER_START):
            instruction = env.sample_real_instruction(rng, env._volume, env._model, env._start_params,
                                                      env._start_agg)
            weights = instruction.get("weights") or {}
            goal_rows.append(instruction["goal"])
            weight_rows.append([weights.get(c, 1.0) for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES] + [weights.get("other", 1.0)])
            kinds.append(instruction["kind"])
        out["hist"].append(np.asarray(env._model.histogram, np.float32))
        out["solo"].append(np.asarray(env._solo_max_log(env._volume, env._model), np.float32))
        out["ctrl"].append(ctrl.astype(np.float32))
        out["s0"].append(state_vector(env._start_agg))
        out["act"].append(np.asarray(actions, np.float32))
        out["s1"].append(np.asarray(states, np.float32))
        out["useless"].append(np.asarray(useless, bool))
        out["goal"].append(np.asarray(goal_rows, np.float32))
        out["weights"].append(np.asarray(weight_rows, np.float32))
        out["kind"].append(kinds)
        out["volume"].append(env._volume)
    return {k: np.array(v) for k, v in out.items()}


def collect(seeds, workers: int = 3, actions_per_start: int = ACTIONS_PER_START,
            policy_path: str = None, volume_ids=None, start_variety: float = 0.0,
            task_version: str = "v1") -> dict:
    """`collect_starts` over `workers` processes. `start_variety` is
    `OneShotEnv`'s: that share of starts dims soft tissue and raises the organs,
    so the model also sees states where organs are visible."""
    seeds = list(seeds)
    chunks = [(seeds[i::workers * 4], actions_per_start, policy_path, volume_ids, start_variety, task_version)
              for i in range(workers * 4)]
    chunks = [chunk for chunk in chunks if chunk[0]]
    with mp.get_context("spawn").Pool(workers) as pool:
        parts = pool.map(_collect_chunk, chunks)
    return {k: np.concatenate([p[k] for p in parts]) for k in parts[0]}


def load_data(paths) -> dict:
    """Concatenate `collect` outputs; keys only some files carry (older files
    lack "volume") are dropped, and weights are padded to the n + 1 column
    layout ("other" = 1.0) when files differ."""
    parts = [dict(np.load(p, allow_pickle=True)) for p in paths]
    if len({p["weights"].shape[-1] for p in parts}) > 1:
        for p in parts:
            if p["weights"].shape[-1] == N_CLASSES:
                p["weights"] = np.concatenate([p["weights"], np.ones(p["weights"].shape[:-1] + (1,), np.float32)], -1)
    keys = set.intersection(*(set(p) for p in parts))
    return {k: np.concatenate([p[k] for p in parts]) for k in parts[0] if k in keys}


# --- training -----------------------------------------------------------------

def _tensor(x):
    return torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(x), dtype=torch.float32)


def _context(data) -> torch.Tensor:
    return _tensor(np.concatenate([data["hist"], data["solo"], data["ctrl"], data["s0"]], 1))


def _transitions(data, starts, context):
    per_start = data["act"].shape[1]
    return (context[starts].repeat_interleave(per_start, 0),
            _tensor(data["act"][starts].reshape(-1, ACTION_SIZE)),
            _tensor((data["s1"][starts] - data["s0"][starts][:, None]).reshape(-1, STATE_SIZE)),
            _tensor(data["useless"][starts].reshape(-1).astype(np.float32)))


def split_starts(n_starts: int, validation_share: float = 0.1):
    """A fixed (seed-independent) held-out share of starts for the model's own validation."""
    perm = np.random.default_rng(0).permutation(n_starts)
    cut = max(1, int(n_starts * validation_share)) if n_starts > 1 else 0
    return perm[cut:], perm[:cut]


def train_dynamics(data, train_starts, val_starts, epochs: int = 60, seed: int = 0, log=print):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    context = _context(data)
    ctx, act, delta, useless = _transitions(data, train_starts, context)
    ensemble = DynamicsEnsemble()
    inputs = torch.cat([ctx, act], -1)
    ensemble.mean.copy_(inputs.mean(0))
    ensemble.scale.copy_(inputs.std(0) + 1e-3)
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(ensemble.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-5)
    schedule = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, epochs)
    report = {}
    for epoch in range(epochs):
        ensemble.train()
        for k in range(len(ensemble.nets)):
            order = torch.randint(0, len(act), (len(act),))      # bootstrap per member
            for batch in torch.split(order, 512):
                pred = ensemble(ctx[batch], act[batch])[k]
                loss = (nn.functional.smooth_l1_loss(pred[:, :STATE_SIZE], delta[batch], beta=0.1)
                        + 0.1 * nn.functional.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(pred[:, STATE_SIZE], useless[batch]))
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
        schedule.step()
        if len(val_starts) and (epoch % 10 == 9 or epoch == epochs - 1):
            report = dynamics_report(ensemble, data, val_starts, context)
            log(f"[model_based] dynamics epoch {epoch + 1}: {report}")
    ensemble.eval()
    for parameter in ensemble.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad_(False)
    return ensemble, report


def dynamics_report(ensemble, data, val_starts, context=None) -> dict:
    """Held-out fit, and -- the number that matters -- how well the model ranks
    real attainment for (start, action, instruction) triples it never saw."""
    context = _context(data) if context is None else context
    ctx, act, delta, _ = _transitions(data, val_starts, context)
    per_start = data["act"].shape[1]
    with torch.no_grad():
        pred = ensemble(ctx, act).mean(0)[:, :STATE_SIZE]
        r2 = 1 - ((pred - delta) ** 2).mean(0) / delta.var(0).clamp_min(1e-9)
        pick = torch.randint(0, data["goal"].shape[1], (len(act),), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(0))
        rows = torch.arange(len(act))
        goal = _tensor(data["goal"][val_starts]).repeat_interleave(per_start, 0)[rows, pick]
        weights = _tensor(data["weights"][val_starts]).repeat_interleave(per_start, 0)[rows, pick]
        start = goal_distance(goal, weights, torch.zeros_like(delta))
        keep = start > 1e-6
        real = (1 - goal_distance(goal, weights, delta) / start.clamp_min(1e-6))[keep].numpy()
        guess = (1 - goal_distance(goal, weights, pred) / start.clamp_min(1e-6))[keep].numpy()
    rank = lambda v: np.argsort(np.argsort(v))
    return {"r2_log_vis": round(float(r2[:N_CLASSES + 1].mean()), 3),
            "r2_bright": round(float(r2[N_CLASSES + 1:2 * N_CLASSES + 1].mean()), 3),
            "attainment_spearman": round(float(np.corrcoef(rank(real), rank(guess))[0, 1]), 3),
            "attainment_mae": round(float(np.abs(np.clip(real, -2, 1) - np.clip(guess, -2, 1)).mean()), 3)}


def train_policy(ensemble, data, train_starts, steps: int = 6000, beta: float = 1.0,
                 useless_weight: float = 1.0, seed: int = 0, batch: int = 256, hidden: int = 256,
                 depth: int = 2, learning_rate: float = 3e-4, cosine: bool = False, log=print):
    """Fit the actor by gradient descent on the model-predicted normalised goal
    distance (1 - attainment, capped at 3 so hopeless instructions do not
    dominate), plus `beta` x ensemble disagreement and `useless_weight` x the
    predicted probability of a useless render."""
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    context, goal_all, weights_all = _context(data), _tensor(data["goal"]), _tensor(data["weights"])
    hist, solo, ctrl, s0 = (_tensor(data[k]) for k in ("hist", "solo", "ctrl", "s0"))
    net = ModelBasedPolicy.new_net(hidden, depth)
    policy = ModelBasedPolicy(net.train())
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(net.parameters(), lr=learning_rate)
    schedule = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, steps) if cosine else None
    per_start = data["goal"].shape[1]
    model_attainment = None
    for step in range(steps):
        starts = torch.as_tensor(rng.choice(train_starts, batch))
        pick = torch.as_tensor(rng.integers(0, per_start, batch))
        goal, weights = goal_all[starts, pick], weights_all[starts, pick]
        start = goal_distance(goal, weights, torch.zeros(batch, STATE_SIZE))
        keep = start > 1e-6
        obs = policy_observation(goal, hist[starts], s0[starts], solo[starts], ctrl[starts])
        action = (ctrl[starts] + policy.action_tensor(obs)).clamp(-1, 1) - ctrl[starts]
        pred = ensemble(context[starts], action)
        mean = pred.mean(0)
        normalised = goal_distance(goal, weights, mean[:, :STATE_SIZE]) / start.clamp_min(1e-6)
        disagreement = pred[:, :, :STATE_SIZE].std(0).mean(1)
        loss = (normalised.clamp(max=3.0) + beta * disagreement
                + useless_weight * torch.sigmoid(mean[:, STATE_SIZE]))[keep].mean()
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        if schedule is not None:
            schedule.step()
        if step % 1000 == 999:
            model_attainment = float((1 - normalised[keep]).median().detach())
            log(f"[model_based] policy step {step + 1}: model-predicted median attainment "
                f"{model_attainment:+.3f}, disagreement {float(disagreement.mean().detach()):.3f}")
    net.eval()
    return policy, {"model_predicted_median": model_attainment}


def evaluate(policy, episodes: int = 200, seed_base: int = VALIDATION_SEED_BASE, split: str = "val",
             volume_ids=None, model_for_volume=None, task_version: str = "v1") -> dict:
    """The policy alone on real renders: one `OneShotEnv` step per episode."""
    import datasets
    from rl.oneshot_env import OneShotEnv
    ids = volume_ids if volume_ids is not None else datasets.volumes_for_split(split)
    kwargs = {} if model_for_volume is None else {"model_for_volume": model_for_volume}
    env = OneShotEnv(ids, **kwargs, **ENV_KWARGS, task_version=task_version)
    rows, by_kind = [], collections.defaultdict(list)
    for seed in range(seed_base, seed_base + episodes):
        obs, _ = env.reset(seed=seed)
        action, _ = policy.predict(obs)
        _, _, _, _, info = env.step(action)
        rows.append({"seed": seed, "kind": info["kind"], "attainment": info["attainment"],
                     "solvable": info["solvable"], "useless": info["useless"]})
        by_kind[info["kind"]].append(info["attainment"])
        by_kind["ALL"].append(info["attainment"])
    return {"medians": {k: float(np.median(v)) for k, v in sorted(by_kind.items())},
            "useless_share": float(np.mean([r["useless"] for r in rows])), "rows": rows}


def run_training(data_paths, out: str, seed: int = 0, model_epochs: int = 60, policy_steps: int = 6000,
                 beta: float = 1.0, useless_weight: float = 1.0, eval_episodes: int = 200,
                 dynamics_path: str = None, hidden: int = 256, depth: int = 2,
                 learning_rate: float = 3e-4, cosine: bool = False, task_version: str = "v1",
                 log=print) -> dict:
    """Fit the dynamics ensemble (or reuse the one in `dynamics_path`, a
    checkpoint.pt trained on the same data), then the policy through it."""
    data = load_data(data_paths)
    train_starts, val_starts = split_starts(len(data["s0"]))
    t0 = time.time()
    if dynamics_path:
        ensemble = load_dynamics(dynamics_path)
        for parameter in ensemble.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad_(False)
        dyn_report = {"reused": dynamics_path, **dynamics_report(ensemble, data, val_starts)}
        log(f"[model_based] reused dynamics: {dyn_report}")
    else:
        ensemble, dyn_report = train_dynamics(data, train_starts, val_starts, epochs=model_epochs,
                                              seed=seed, log=log)
    policy, pol_report = train_policy(ensemble, data, train_starts, steps=policy_steps, beta=beta,
                                      useless_weight=useless_weight, seed=seed, hidden=hidden,
                                      depth=depth, learning_rate=learning_rate, cosine=cosine, log=log)
    policy.metadata = {"seed": seed, "data": list(data_paths), "starts": int(len(data["s0"])),
                       "model_epochs": model_epochs, "policy_steps": policy_steps, "beta": beta,
                       "useless_weight": useless_weight, "hidden": hidden, "depth": depth,
                       "learning_rate": learning_rate, "cosine": cosine, "dynamics": dynamics_path,
                       "task_version": task_version}
    report = {"dynamics": dyn_report, "policy": pol_report, "train_seconds": round(time.time() - t0)}
    if eval_episodes:
        result = evaluate(policy, episodes=eval_episodes, task_version=task_version)
        report["val"] = {"medians": result["medians"], "useless_share": result["useless_share"]}
        log(f"[model_based] real val medians: {result['medians']}")
        os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
        with open(os.path.join(out, "eval_val.json"), "w") as stream:
            json.dump(result, stream, indent=1)
    path = policy.save(out, ensemble, report)
    log(f"[model_based] wrote {path}")
    return {"policy": policy, "dynamics": ensemble, "report": report, "path": path}


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    c = sub.add_parser("collect", help="render random (and policy) TF changes on training-split starts")
    c.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    c.add_argument("--starts", type=int, default=3000)
    c.add_argument("--seed-base", type=int, default=1_000_000)
    c.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=3)
    c.add_argument("--actions", type=int, default=ACTIONS_PER_START)
    c.add_argument("--policy", default=None, help="checkpoint.pt whose proposals fill half the actions (Dyna)")
    c.add_argument("--splits", default="train",
                   help="comma-separated volume splits to draw starts from, e.g. train,train_extra")
    c.add_argument("--task-version", choices=goals.TASK_VERSIONS, default="v1")
    c.add_argument("--start-variety", type=float, default=0.0,
                   help="share of starts with soft tissue dimmed and organs raised (OneShotEnv)")
    t = sub.add_parser("train", help="fit the dynamics ensemble, then the policy through it")
    t.add_argument("--data", required=True, help="comma-separated .npz files from `collect`")
    t.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    t.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    t.add_argument("--model-epochs", type=int, default=60)
    t.add_argument("--policy-steps", type=int, default=6000)
    t.add_argument("--beta", type=float, default=1.0)
    t.add_argument("--useless-weight", type=float, default=1.0)
    t.add_argument("--eval-episodes", type=int, default=200)
    t.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=3)
    t.add_argument("--dynamics", default=None, help="reuse this checkpoint.pt's dynamics ensemble")
    t.add_argument("--policy-hidden", type=int, default=256)
    t.add_argument("--policy-depth", type=int, default=2)
    t.add_argument("--policy-lr", type=float, default=3e-4)
    t.add_argument("--policy-cosine", action="store_true", help="cosine-anneal the policy learning rate")
    t.add_argument("--task-version", choices=goals.TASK_VERSIONS, default="v1")
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "collect":
        import datasets
        volume_ids = [name for split in args.splits.split(",") for name in datasets.volumes_for_split(split)]
        data = collect(range(args.seed_base, args.seed_base + args.starts), workers=args.workers,
                       volume_ids=volume_ids,
                       actions_per_start=args.actions, policy_path=args.policy,
                       start_variety=args.start_variety, task_version=args.task_version)
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.out)), exist_ok=True)
        np.savez_compressed(args.out, **data)
        print(f"[model_based] wrote {args.out}: {len(data['s0'])} starts x {data['act'].shape[1]} actions")
    else:
        torch.set_num_threads(args.threads)
        run_training(args.data.split(","), args.out, seed=args.seed, model_epochs=args.model_epochs,
                     policy_steps=args.policy_steps, beta=args.beta, useless_weight=args.useless_weight,
                     eval_episodes=args.eval_episodes, dynamics_path=args.dynamics,
                     hidden=args.policy_hidden, depth=args.policy_depth, learning_rate=args.policy_lr,
                     cosine=args.policy_cosine, task_version=args.task_version)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### rl/oneshot_env.py
```
"""Gymnasium environment: output a transfer function in one shot to follow a
visibility instruction.

Each episode picks a volume and an instruction (`goals.sample_instruction`);
unlike `vis_env.VisibilityTFEnv`'s ten-step edits, here the single action *is*
the new value of each controllable parameter group
(`rl.baselines.CONTROLLABLE`: height, width and colour per peak -- centres
stay fixed), not a delta, and the episode ends immediately. The reward is the
attainment of the resulting transfer function (`goals.attainment`), clipped
to `[-1, 1]`, minus a penalty when the result shows effectively nothing.

See docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-16-rl-v2-plan6-oneshot.md (at tag pre-cleanup-2026-10-19) for why: the
ten-step formulation asks the policy to learn a blind search procedure, which
did not work, while amortising a single forward pass over the mapping from
(instruction, start state) to transfer function does.
"""
import math
import json
import os

import gymnasium as gym
import numpy as np
from gymnasium import spaces

import goals
import transfer
import visibility
from anatomy import LAYOUT_VERSION
from anatomy_layers import default_layers, normalize_layers
from rl.baselines import CONTROLLABLE, apply_controllable, hill_climb

N_GOAL_CLASSES = len(goals.GOAL_CLASSES)
OBSERVATION_SIZE = 4 * N_GOAL_CLASSES + 16 + 3 * N_GOAL_CLASSES + len(CONTROLLABLE) + 1
ACTION_SIZE = len(CONTROLLABLE)
POLICY_VERSION = "oneshot-v6"
V7_POLICY_VERSION = "oneshot-v7"
V8_POLICY_VERSION = "oneshot-v8"
# v9: v7's residual/target contract on the label-scoped transfer function
# (transfer.DEFAULT_TRANSFER_MODE) -- each organ's peak shades only voxels
# segmented as that organ, so no layer-opacity actions are needed.
V9_POLICY_VERSION = "oneshot-v9"
RESIDUAL_POLICY_VERSIONS = (V7_POLICY_VERSION, V8_POLICY_VERSION, V9_POLICY_VERSION)
POLICY_VERSIONS = (POLICY_VERSION,) + RESIDUAL_POLICY_VERSIONS
LAYER_NAMES = tuple(goals.GOAL_CLASSES)
V8_OBSERVATION_SIZE = OBSERVATION_SIZE + len(LAYER_NAMES)
V8_ACTION_SIZE = ACTION_SIZE + len(LAYER_NAMES)
USELESS_PENALTY = 1.0
REWARD_CLIP = 1.0

# Reset noise (normalized parameter units, clipped to [-1, 1] after adding),
# uniform per controllable group -- matches VisibilityTFEnv's reset noise.
START_NOISE = 0.3

# Start variety (opt-in, `start_variety`): with this share of resets, the
# start draws soft tissue nearly transparent and the organs more opaque, so
# organs that the anatomical start hides behind soft tissue are visible and
# can be asked about. On the training split this roughly doubles how often
# a start shows heart/vessels/kidneys/spleen at vis >= goals.EPSILON
# (kidneys 16 -> 30 %, spleen 19 -> 44 %, vessels 22 -> 45 %, heart 34 -> 60 %,
# 80-120 starts each), without making any start useless. Redrawing *every*
# height uniformly instead made organs rarer (kidneys 1 %), since a dense
# skeleton or soft-tissue peak then hides them again. Unit heights, like
# transfer.ANATOMICAL_HEIGHTS.
START_VARIETY_SOFT_HEIGHT = (0.0, 0.15)
START_VARIETY_ORGAN_HEIGHT = (0.25, 0.7)
START_VARIETY_ORGANS = ("heart", "vessels", "liver", "kidneys", "spleen")

# Hindsight target noise, in the same normalized parameter units as
# START_NOISE: the target transfer function is the start's controllable values
# plus U(-HINDSIGHT_NOISE, HINDSIGHT_NOISE) per group, not a uniform draw over
# the whole action space. A uniform draw lands far from the start and asks for
# visibility changes of a factor of ~40 (median 1.62 log10, up to the full 3.0
# floor-to-visible range), while a real instruction asks for 0.15 to 1.0
# (goals.VISIBILITY_STRENGTH and goals.HIDE_STRENGTH) -- training on the former
# and evaluating on the latter is a goal-distribution shift. It also moves
# every peak at once, including those of the classes the goal does not mention,
# which goals.distance then charges as keep-term drift, so the target itself
# could not score near 1.
#
# 0.25 is calibrated, not round: swept over 200 synthetic episodes each, the
# median largest |delta| runs 0.19 (s=0.1), 0.36 (0.2), 0.44 (0.25), 0.53
# (0.3), 0.97 (0.5). 0.25 puts the median inside the band real instructions
# ask for and at the plateau of the share that lands in [0.15, 1.0] (62%,
# against 63% at 0.3 and 52% at 0.15), while giving the cleanest oracle --
# median attainment 0.985 for the action that produced the target, against
# 0.729 under the uniform draw this replaced.
HINDSIGHT_NOISE = 0.25

# Brightness hindsight: move one visible class's colour group by up to this
# much (normalized units, so up to 0.4 in unit brightness -- the "strongly"
# end of goals.BRIGHTNESS_STRENGTH) and nothing else, so the demonstrated
# action changes that class's brightness without charging keep drift.
HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_NOISE = 0.8
HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MIN_DELTA = 0.05
# Brightness is only measured on visible tissue (visibility.features reports
# 0 below 1e-4), so a class needs this much visibility to be demonstrable.
HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MIN_VIS = 1e-3

# Show-only hindsight: push 1-2 classes' height *up from wherever it
# already is* and every other class's height *down from wherever it
# already is* -- a relative nudge, mirroring brightness's own pattern, not
# an absolute snap to a fixed region. An absolute region can't work here:
# transfer.ANATOMICAL_HEIGHTS ranges from 0.05 (lungs) to 0.60 (skeleton),
# so any single fixed "low" region sits above most classes' own starting
# height, which would push a "hidden" class brighter instead of dimmer.
# Randomized magnitude, not a fixed snap to one offset, since overlapping
# anatomy means a literal full separation isn't always reachable or
# realistic.
HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_PUSH_RANGE = (0.4, 1.2)
HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_MIN_DELTA = 0.05
# Requiring every shown class *and* every hidden class to clear its own
# threshold in the same try is a harder simultaneous condition than
# brightness's single one-sided check on one freely-re-picked class --
# measured at ~1.1-1.2% fallback-to-None on fake_a/_StubModel over 2000
# seeds against the shared HINDSIGHT_MAX_TRIES=20 (mean 2.48 tries to
# succeed, long tail), vs. 0% for brightness under the same budget. A
# dedicated, larger budget keeps that fallback rate low without touching
# brightness's or the generic sampler's own tuning.
HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_MAX_TRIES = 50

# Distance already contains the keep penalty for unmentioned classes. Keep
# auxiliary drift small so target progress remains primary signal instead of
# double-penalizing ordinary occlusion side effects.
TARGET_DRIFT_WEIGHT = 0.1

# Generous finite bound for the observation Box: every packed quantity (goal
# components, log-vis/brightness within a handful of units, start params and
# coverage in/near [-1, 1]) stays well inside it.
OBSERVATION_BOUND = 10.0

# Below this, distance(goal, start, start) is ~0 (the instruction asks for
# effectively nothing) and attainment's ratio is meaningless -- report 0
# instead of dividing by ~0.
_ATTAINMENT_FLOOR = 1e-9


def resolve_policy_metadata(metadata=None) -> dict:
    values = {"policy_version": POLICY_VERSION, "action_mode": "absolute",
              "reward_mode": "attainment"}
    if metadata:
        values.update({key: metadata[key] for key in values if key in metadata})
        # Only a v2 task is recorded, so every v1 checkpoint's metadata (and the
        # env kwargs built from it) stays exactly as it was.
        if metadata.get("task_version", "v1") != "v1":
            if metadata["task_version"] not in goals.TASK_VERSIONS:
                raise ValueError(f"unsupported task version: {metadata['task_version']}")
            values["task_version"] = metadata["task_version"]
    if values["policy_version"] not in POLICY_VERSIONS:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported policy version: {values['policy_version']}")
    if values["policy_version"] == V9_POLICY_VERSION and metadata and \
            metadata.get("transfer_mode", transfer.DEFAULT_TRANSFER_MODE) != transfer.DEFAULT_TRANSFER_MODE:
        raise ValueError(f"oneshot-v9 checkpoint uses transfer mode {metadata['transfer_mode']!r}, "
                         f"this build evaluates {transfer.DEFAULT_TRANSFER_MODE!r}")
    if values["policy_version"] == POLICY_VERSION and values["action_mode"] != "absolute":
        raise ValueError("v6 only supports absolute action mode")
    if values["policy_version"] == POLICY_VERSION and values["reward_mode"] != "attainment":
        raise ValueError("v6 only supports attainment reward")
    if values["action_mode"] not in ("absolute", "residual"):
        raise ValueError("action_mode must be absolute or residual")
    if values["reward_mode"] not in ("attainment", "target"):
        raise ValueError("reward_mode must be attainment or target")
    if values["policy_version"] == V7_POLICY_VERSION and (
            values["action_mode"] != "residual" or values["reward_mode"] != "target"):
        raise ValueError("oneshot-v7 requires residual actions and target reward")
    if values["policy_version"] in (V8_POLICY_VERSION, V9_POLICY_VERSION) and (
            values["action_mode"] != "residual" or values["reward_mode"] != "target"):
        raise ValueError(f"{values['policy_version']} requires residual actions and target reward")
    return values


def load_policy_metadata(checkpoint_path: str) -> dict:
    path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(checkpoint_path)), "metadata.json")
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return resolve_policy_metadata()
    with open(path) as stream:
        return resolve_policy_metadata(json.load(stream))


def observation_metadata(policy_version: str = POLICY_VERSION, action_mode: str = "absolute",
                         reward_mode: str = "attainment", task_version: str = "v1") -> dict:
    """Return the serialized contract shared by training and inference."""
    resolved = resolve_policy_metadata({"policy_version": policy_version,
                                        "action_mode": action_mode,
                                        "reward_mode": reward_mode})
    metadata = {
        "policy_version": resolved["policy_version"],
        "anatomy_layout": LAYOUT_VERSION,
        "observation_size": OBSERVATION_SIZE,
        "action_size": V8_ACTION_SIZE if resolved["policy_version"] == V8_POLICY_VERSION else ACTION_SIZE,
        "goal_classes": list(goals.GOAL_CLASSES),
        "controllable_groups": len(CONTROLLABLE),
        **({"action_mode": action_mode, "reward_mode": reward_mode}
           if policy_version in RESIDUAL_POLICY_VERSIONS else {}),
    }
    if resolved["policy_version"] == V9_POLICY_VERSION:
        metadata["transfer_mode"] = transfer.DEFAULT_TRANSFER_MODE
    if task_version != "v1":
        if task_version not in goals.TASK_VERSIONS:
            raise ValueError(f"unsupported task version: {task_version}")
        metadata["task_version"] = task_version
    if resolved["policy_version"] == V8_POLICY_VERSION:
        metadata["observation_size"] = V8_OBSERVATION_SIZE
        metadata["anatomy_layers"] = list(LAYER_NAMES)
    return metadata


def build_observation(goal, histogram, start_agg: dict, solo_max_log, controllable,
                      layer_opacity=None) -> np.ndarray:
    """One-shot observation: goal (4n) + histogram (16) + log10 visibility (n)
    + brightness (n) + log10 solo_max ceiling (n) + controllable start
    parameters + coverage (1), all at
    the start state. The single implementation of this layout -- used by
    `OneShotEnv._build_observation` during training/rollout and by
    `rl.candidates` to query a policy standalone for preference collection,
    so the two can never silently drift apart and feed the policy a
    different observation than it was trained on."""
    log_vis = [math.log10(start_agg["vis"][c] + goals.EPSILON) for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES]
    bright = [start_agg["bright"][c] for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES]
    values = (list(goal) + list(histogram) + log_vis + bright
              + list(solo_max_log) + list(controllable) + [start_agg["coverage"]])
    if layer_opacity is not None:
        values.extend(layer_opacity)
    return np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float32)


class OneShotEnv(gym.Env):
    """One-step episode: the action is the new transfer function, scored by
    how much closer it gets to a sampled visibility/brightness instruction on
    a randomly chosen volume from `volume_ids`.

    Observation layout derives from `len(goals.GOAL_CLASSES)` and
    `len(CONTROLLABLE)`.
    """

    metadata = {"render_modes": []}

    HINDSIGHT_MAX_MENTIONS = 2

    # A hindsight target whose largest class delta is below this asks for
    # effectively nothing: the goal is trivially already met, `_start_distance`
    # sits at the attainment floor, and the episode carries no gradient. Resample
    # instead. 0.05 log10 units is a factor of ~1.12 -- a third of the smallest
    # spoken strength ("slightly", 0.15), so nothing an instruction could ask
    # for is excluded.
    HINDSIGHT_MIN_DELTA = 0.05
    HINDSIGHT_MAX_TRIES = 20

    def __init__(self, volume_ids, model_for_volume=visibility.for_volume,
                 hindsight_ratio: float = 0.0, policy_version: str = POLICY_VERSION,
                 action_mode: str = "absolute", reward_mode: str = "attainment",
                 balance_classes: bool = False, hindsight_brightness_share: float = 0.0,
                 hindsight_show_only_share: float = 0.0, solvable_instructions: bool = False,
                 start_variety: float = 0.0, target_drift_weight: float = TARGET_DRIFT_WEIGHT,
                 task_version: str = "v1"):
        super().__init__()
        if task_version not in goals.TASK_VERSIONS:
            raise ValueError(f"unsupported task version: {task_version}")
        # Task v2 (goals.py, docs/experiments/2026-10-17-task-v2.md): organs
        # visible at the start, well-posed instructions only, occluders exempt
        # from the keep terms, every target clipped to what its class can reach.
        self.task_version = task_version
        goals.use_task_version(task_version)
        if not volume_ids:
            raise ValueError("volume_ids must not be empty")
        self.volume_ids = list(volume_ids)
        self._model_for_volume = model_for_volume
        if policy_version not in POLICY_VERSIONS:
            raise ValueError("unsupported one-shot policy version")
        if policy_version == POLICY_VERSION and action_mode != "absolute":
            raise ValueError("v6 only supports absolute action mode")
        if policy_version in RESIDUAL_POLICY_VERSIONS and action_mode != "residual":
            raise ValueError(f"{policy_version} requires residual action mode")
        if action_mode not in ("absolute", "residual"):
            raise ValueError("action_mode must be absolute or residual")
        if reward_mode not in ("attainment", "target"):
            raise ValueError("reward_mode must be attainment or target")
        if policy_version in RESIDUAL_POLICY_VERSIONS and reward_mode != "target":
            raise ValueError(f"{policy_version} requires target reward")
        if not 0.0 <= hindsight_ratio <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("hindsight_ratio must be between 0 and 1")
        if not 0.0 <= hindsight_brightness_share <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("hindsight_brightness_share must be between 0 and 1")
        if not 0.0 <= hindsight_show_only_share <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("hindsight_show_only_share must be between 0 and 1")
        # Share of hindsight episodes whose goal is a brightness change
        # (`_sample_hindsight_brightness`) rather than a visibility change.
        self.hindsight_brightness_share = float(hindsight_brightness_share)
        # Share of hindsight episodes whose goal is a show-only change
        # (`_sample_hindsight_show_only`) rather than a visibility change.
        self.hindsight_show_only_share = float(hindsight_show_only_share)
        self.policy_version = policy_version
        self.action_mode = action_mode
        self.reward_mode = reward_mode
        self.balance_classes = balance_classes
        # Sample only relative/compound targets the start state allows
        # (goals.sample_instruction(solvable_only=True)). Always on in task v2/v3.
        self.solvable_instructions = bool(solvable_instructions) or task_version != "v1"
        if not 0.0 <= start_variety <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("start_variety must be between 0 and 1")
        self.start_variety = float(start_variety)
        if target_drift_weight < 0.0:
            raise ValueError("target_drift_weight must not be negative")
        self.target_drift_weight = float(target_drift_weight)
        self._class_counts = {}
        # A sampled instruction can be unanswerable on the volume it lands on
        # ("a bit more lungs" on a pelvis scan, where the lung ceiling is a
        # fraction of a percent of the image): the episode teaches nothing and
        # pollutes evaluation. This share of episodes inverts the sampling
        # instead -- draw a *reachable* target transfer function, measure what
        # it achieved, and make that the goal -- so every such episode has a
        # demonstrated solution, the action that produced the target.
        self.hindsight_ratio = float(hindsight_ratio)
        self._model_cache = {}
        self._solo_max_log_cache = {}

        observation_size = V8_OBSERVATION_SIZE if policy_version == V8_POLICY_VERSION else OBSERVATION_SIZE
        action_size = V8_ACTION_SIZE if policy_version == V8_POLICY_VERSION else ACTION_SIZE
        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
            low=-OBSERVATION_BOUND, high=OBSERVATION_BOUND, shape=(observation_size,), dtype=np.float32)
        self.action_space = spaces.Box(low=-1.0, high=1.0, shape=(action_size,), dtype=np.float32)

        self._volume = None
        self._model = None
        self._start_params = None
        self._params = None
        self._instruction = None
        self._start_agg = None
        self._start_distance = None
        # Set by reset() in both branches, so a hindsight episode can never
        # leave its oracle action behind for a later instruction episode.
        self._hindsight_action = None

    def contract_metadata(self) -> dict:
        return observation_metadata(self.policy_version, self.action_mode, self.reward_mode,
                                    self.task_version)

    def _get_model(self, volume: str):
        # visibility.for_volume() already caches to disk; this in-memory cache
        # additionally avoids re-reading/re-decompressing that cache file on
        # every one of the many resets a training run does.
        model = self._model_cache.get(volume)
        if model is None:
            model = self._model_for_volume(volume)
            self._model_cache[volume] = model
        return model

    def _solo_max_log(self, volume: str, model) -> list:
        """log10(ceiling + EPSILON) per `goals.GOAL_CLASSES`, where ceiling is
        the most of that goal class a single-peak transfer function can show
        (`model.solo_max`, summed over `goals.MEASURED_FOR_GOAL` the way
        `goals._absolute_target_delta` does, since a goal class like "soft"
        has no single underlying `model.solo_max` name of its own).
        `model.solo_max` already caches per class on the model, but this
        additionally avoids redoing the summation/log10 work on every reset
        of an already-seen volume.
        """
        layers = getattr(self, "_active_layers", None)
        cache_key = volume if layers is None else (volume, tuple(
            (name, settings.get("opacity", 1.0))
            for name, settings in layers.items()))
        cached = self._solo_max_log_cache.get(cache_key)
        if cached is None:
            cached = [math.log10(goals.class_ceiling(model, c, layers) + goals.EPSILON)
                      for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES]
            self._solo_max_log_cache[cache_key] = cached
        return cached

    def _sample_start_params(self, rng) -> np.ndarray:
        params = goals.starting_params(self.task_version)
        for group in CONTROLLABLE:
            noise = rng.uniform(-START_NOISE, START_NOISE)
            for index in group:
                params[index] = params[index] + noise
        # Draw only when enabled, so the default path's RNG stream (and with
        # it every existing seeded episode) is unchanged.
        if self.start_variety > 0.0 and rng.random() < self.start_variety:
            self._vary_start(params, rng)
        return np.clip(params, -1.0, 1.0)

    @staticmethod
    def _vary_start(params: np.ndarray, rng) -> None:
        """Soft tissue nearly transparent, organs more opaque (START_VARIETY_*)."""
        def set_height(goal_class, low, high):
            index = transfer.ANATOMICAL_PEAK_INDEX[goal_class] * transfer.PARAMS_PER_PEAK + 2
            params[index] = rng.uniform(low, high) * 2.0 - 1.0
        set_height("soft", *START_VARIETY_SOFT_HEIGHT)
        for goal_class in START_VARIETY_ORGANS:
            set_height(goal_class, *START_VARIETY_ORGAN_HEIGHT)

    def _controllable_values(self, params: np.ndarray) -> list:
        return [float(np.mean([params[i] for i in group])) for group in CONTROLLABLE]

    # `self._instruction`/`self._start_agg`/etc. are set to None in __init__
    # and only ever read here, after reset() has always already filled them
    # in -- the standard Gymnasium convention (reset() before step()), not a
    # real possibility of None reaching these lines.
    def _build_observation(self) -> np.ndarray:
        solo_max_log = self._solo_max_log(self._volume, self._model)
        controllable = self._controllable_values(self._start_params)
        layer_opacity = None
        if self.policy_version == V8_POLICY_VERSION:
            layer_opacity = [self._active_layers[name]["opacity"] for name in LAYER_NAMES]
        return build_observation(self._instruction["goal"], self._model.histogram, self._start_agg,  # pyright: ignore[reportOptionalSubscript]
                                   solo_max_log, controllable, layer_opacity)

    def _features(self, params):
        return goals.features(self._model, params, getattr(self, "_active_layers", None))

    def _vis(self, params):
        return goals.vis(self._model, params, getattr(self, "_active_layers", None))

    def _attainment(self, agg: dict) -> float:
        if self._start_distance <= _ATTAINMENT_FLOOR:
            return 0.0
        return goals.attainment(self._instruction["goal"], self._start_agg, agg,  # pyright: ignore[reportOptionalSubscript]
                                weights=self._instruction.get("weights"))  # pyright: ignore[reportOptionalSubscript]

    def _info(self, attainment: float, useless: bool) -> dict:
        info = {"attainment": attainment, "kind": self._instruction["kind"],  # pyright: ignore[reportOptionalSubscript]
                "volume": self._volume, "text": self._instruction["text"], "useless": useless,  # pyright: ignore[reportOptionalSubscript]
                "goal_source": "hindsight" if self._hindsight_action is not None else "instruction",
                "solvable": getattr(self, "_solvable", None)}
        if self.policy_version in (V7_POLICY_VERSION, V9_POLICY_VERSION):
            info["policy_version"] = self.policy_version
        return info

    def hindsight_action(self):
        """The action that produced this episode's target, or None when the
        episode came from an instruction. Test and distillation hook."""
        return None if self._hindsight_action is None else self._hindsight_action.copy()

    def hindsight_policy_action(self):
        """The hindsight oracle in the policy's own action space: the absolute
        controllable values for "absolute" mode, their offset from the start
        state for "residual" mode (layer actions, v8 only, left at 0 -- no
        change). None for an instruction episode. The supervised target for
        `rl.pretrain`."""
        if self._hindsight_action is None:
            return None
        action = self._hindsight_action.copy()
        if self.action_mode == "residual":
            action = action - np.asarray(self._controllable_values(self._start_params))
        if self.policy_version == V8_POLICY_VERSION:
            action = np.concatenate([action, np.zeros(len(LAYER_NAMES))])
        return action.astype(np.float32)

    def search_policy_action(self, evaluations: int = 200, initial_step: float = 0.2):
        """A `hill_climb` search solution for this episode's real instruction,
        in the policy's own action space -- same encoding as
        `hindsight_policy_action`. None for a hindsight episode (which
        already has a free oracle action; see `hindsight_policy_action`).
        The supervised target for a search-distilled `rl.pretrain` run."""
        if self._hindsight_action is not None:
            return None
        target_params = hill_climb(self._model, self._start_params, self._instruction,
                                   evaluations=evaluations, initial_step=initial_step,
                                   active_layers=getattr(self, "_active_layers", None))
        action = np.asarray(self._controllable_values(target_params))
        if self.action_mode == "residual":
            action = action - np.asarray(self._controllable_values(self._start_params))
        if self.policy_version == V8_POLICY_VERSION:
            action = np.concatenate([action, np.zeros(len(LAYER_NAMES))])
        return action.astype(np.float32)

    def _sample_hindsight_brightness(self, rng, start_params, start_agg):
        """(instruction-shaped dict, action) for a brightness goal on one
        visible class, or None when no class is visible enough."""
        visible = [c for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES
                   if start_agg["vis"][c] >= HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MIN_VIS]
        if not visible:
            return None
        start_controllable = np.asarray(self._controllable_values(start_params))
        for _ in range(self.HINDSIGHT_MAX_TRIES):
            goal_class = str(rng.choice(visible))
            base = goals.PEAK_INDEX[goal_class] * transfer.PARAMS_PER_PEAK
            group = CONTROLLABLE.index((base + 3, base + 4, base + 5))
            action = start_controllable.copy()
            action[group] = np.clip(action[group] + rng.uniform(-HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_NOISE,
                                                                HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_NOISE), -1.0, 1.0)
            target_agg = goals.aggregate(self._features(apply_controllable(start_params, action)),
                                         self._active_layers)
            delta = target_agg["bright"][goal_class] - start_agg["bright"][goal_class]
            if abs(delta) >= HINDSIGHT_BRIGHTNESS_MIN_DELTA:
                targets = {goal_class: {"bright": float(delta)}}
                return ({"kind": "hindsight", "text": None, "targets": targets,
                         "goal": goals.goal_vector(targets)}, action)
        return None

    def _sample_hindsight_show_only(self, rng, start_params, start_agg):
        """(instruction-shaped dict, action) for a show-only goal: 1-2
        classes pushed toward high visibility, every other class pushed
        toward low -- the one hindsight shape with an explicit target on
        every class, matching goals._sample_show_only's real-instruction
        shape."""
        n = min(int(rng.integers(1, 3)), len(goals.GOAL_CLASSES))
        start_controllable = np.asarray(self._controllable_values(start_params))
        for _ in range(HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_MAX_TRIES):
            shown = set(rng.choice(goals.GOAL_CLASSES, size=n, replace=False))
            hidden = set(goals.GOAL_CLASSES) - shown
            action = start_controllable.copy()
            for goal_class in goals.GOAL_CLASSES:
                base = goals.PEAK_INDEX[goal_class] * transfer.PARAMS_PER_PEAK
                group = CONTROLLABLE.index((base + 2,))
                sign = 1.0 if goal_class in shown else -1.0
                push = rng.uniform(*HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_PUSH_RANGE)
                action[group] = np.clip(action[group] + sign * push, -1.0, 1.0)
            target_agg = goals.aggregate(self._features(apply_controllable(start_params, action)),
                                         self._active_layers)
            deltas = {c: math.log10(target_agg["vis"][c] + goals.EPSILON)
                         - math.log10(start_agg["vis"][c] + goals.EPSILON)
                      for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES}
            shown_ok = min(deltas[c] for c in shown) >= HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_MIN_DELTA
            hidden_ok = max(deltas[c] for c in hidden) <= -HINDSIGHT_SHOW_ONLY_MIN_DELTA
            if shown_ok and hidden_ok:
                targets = {c: {"vis": deltas[c]} for c in goals.GOAL_CLASSES}
                weights = goals.show_only_weights(shown, goals.GOAL_CLASSES)
                return ({"kind": "hindsight", "text": None, "targets": targets,
                         "goal": goals.goal_vector(targets), "weights": weights}, action)
        return None

    def _sample_hindsight_goal(self, rng, model, start_params, start_agg):
        """(instruction-shaped dict, action) from a reachable target."""
        if self.hindsight_brightness_share and rng.random() < self.hindsight_brightness_share:
            sampled = self._sample_hindsight_brightness(rng, start_params, start_agg)
            if sampled is not None:
                return sampled
        if self.hindsight_show_only_share and rng.random() < self.hindsight_show_only_share:
            sampled = self._sample_hindsight_show_only(rng, start_params, start_agg)
            if sampled is not None:
                return sampled
        start_controllable = np.asarray(self._controllable_values(start_params))
        for _ in range(self.HINDSIGHT_MAX_TRIES):
            noise = rng.uniform(-HINDSIGHT_NOISE, HINDSIGHT_NOISE, size=len(CONTROLLABLE))
            action = np.clip(start_controllable + noise, -1.0, 1.0)
            target_params = apply_controllable(start_params, action)
            target_vis_agg = goals.aggregate_vis(self._vis(target_params))

            deltas = {}
            for goal_class in goals.GOAL_CLASSES:
                start_vis = start_agg["vis"][goal_class]
                target_vis = target_vis_agg[goal_class]
                # Same convention as goals.progress, so the goal the policy is
                # handed and the change the reward measures are one quantity.
                deltas[goal_class] = math.log10(target_vis + goals.EPSILON) \
                    - math.log10(start_vis + goals.EPSILON)

            ranked = sorted(goals.GOAL_CLASSES, key=lambda c: -abs(deltas[c]))
            if abs(deltas[ranked[0]]) >= self.HINDSIGHT_MIN_DELTA:
                break
        # A real instruction names one or two classes, never all classes; a
        # hindsight goal that mentioned every class would shift the observation
